Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the cryptography structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 435. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 677 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the cryptography space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 435 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 677. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the cryptography object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 843. The moduli space has dimension at most 879 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the cryptography decomposition. By minimality, the element a^843 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 879-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
